During the review period, the Group successfully signed a 5-year sustainability-       於回顧期內，集團成功與14家主要的國際及
linked loan facility agreement with 14 major international and local banks.            本地銀行簽署一筆五年期的可持續發展表現掛
The syndicated facility received an overwhelming response from the market              鈎貸款協議。本次銀團貸款的市場反應非常熱
and was significantly oversubscribed. The final size of the loan came to HK$7          烈，錄得大幅超額認購，最終貸款額達到70億
billion, reflecting the Group’s efforts towards its sustainability goals, as well as   港元，反映集團致力實現可持續發展目標，以
the banking industry’s trust and support of the Group’s environmental, social          及銀行業界充份信任及支持集團在環境、社會
and governance (ESG) work.                                                             及管治(ESG)方面的工作。

probability of default and loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if     預期信貸虧損之計量是違約概率及違約損失率

amount that is determined with the respective risks of default occurring as the      概率加權金額，該金額以發生違約的相應風險
weights, and also with reference to the time value of money. In determining          為權重釐定，同時亦參考貨幣的時間價值。於

making payments of interest and principal for its indebtedness, and forward-         人拖欠其債務的利息及本金付款以及前瞻性資
looking information including the future macroeconomic conditions at places          料，包括債券發行人現時經營所在地的未來宏
where the bond issuer is operating.                                                  觀經濟狀況。

     Division and responsibilities                                                  分工及職責
     The Board is headed by the Chairman whose role differs from that of            董事會由主席領導，其職務有別於本公
     the Managing Director of the Company. The roles of the Chairman                司董事總經理。主席及董事總經理各有
     and Managing Director are segregated and are not exercised by the              獨立職務，且非由同一名人士擔任。
     same individual.

     The duties of the Chairman include (but not limited to) the following:         主席職責包括但不限於：

     •       scheduling meetings that enable the Board to perform                   •   安排會議以便董事會履行職責，
             its duties and responsibilities and to ensure all key and                  並確保董事會及時討論所有重要

INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’S REPORT
獨立核數師報告




TO THE SHAREHOLDERS OF KINGBOARD LAMINATES HOLDINGS                               致建滔積層板控股有限公司股東
LIMITED
(incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability)                       （於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公司）



OPINION                                                                           意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Kingboard                本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第
Laminates Holdings Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively     69至217頁的建滔集團有限公司（以下簡稱「貴
referred to as “the Group”) set out on pages 69 to 217, which comprise the        公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「貴集團」）的綜合財
consolidated statement of financial position as at 31 December 2022, and          務報表，此財務報表包括於二零二二年十二月
the consolidated statement of profit or loss and the consolidated statement       三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該日止年度
of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, consolidated statement          的綜合損益表及綜合損益及其他全面收益表、
of changes in equity and consolidated statement of cash flows for the year        綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量表，以及綜合
then ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including a       財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策概要。
summary of significant accounting policies.

     •         clarify that if a liability has terms that could, at the option of                            方選擇透過轉讓實體本身之權益

2.   APPLICATION OF AMENDMENTS TO                                                2.   應用經修訂之香港財務
     HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING                                                    申報準則（「香港財務申
     STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (continued)                                                 報準則」）
                                                                                          （續）

     New and amendments to HKFRSs in issue but not yet                                已頒佈但尚未生效之新訂及經修
     effective (continued)                                                            訂香港財務報告準則（續）
     Amendments to HKAS 12 “Deferred Tax related to Assets and                        香港會計準則第12號（修訂本）「 與單一
     Liabilities arising from a Single Transaction”                                   交易產生的資產及負債相關的遞延稅
                                                                                      項」
     The amendments narrow the scope of the recognition exemption                     該等修訂本縮窄香港會計準則第12號
     of deferred tax liabilities and deferred tax assets in paragraphs 15             「所得稅」
                                                                                          （「香港會計準則第12號」）第15
     and 24 of HKAS 12 “Income Taxes” (“HKAS 12”) so that it no longer                及24段遞延稅項負債及遞延稅項資產的
     applies to transactions that, on initial recognition, give rise to equal         確認豁免範圍，使其不再適用於在初始
     taxable and deductible temporary differences.                                    確認時產生相等的應課稅和可抵扣暫時
                                                                                      性差額的交易。

     As disclosed in note 3 to the consolidated financial statements, for             誠如綜合財務報表附註3所披露，就稅
     leasing transactions in which the tax deductions are attributable                項扣減歸屬於租賃負債之租賃交易而
     to the lease liabilities, the Group applies HKAS 12 requirements to              言，本集將香港會計準則第12號之規定
     the relevant assets and liabilities separately. Temporary differences            分別應用於相關資產及負債。由於應用
     on initial recognition of the relevant assets and liabilities are not            初始確認豁免，故不會確認有關資產及
     recognised due to application of the initial recognition exemption.              負債於初始確認時之暫時差額。

     Upon the application of the amendments, the Group will recognise a               於應用該等修訂本後，本集團將確認延
     deferred tax asset (to the extent that it is probable that taxable profit        稅項資產（倘應課稅溢利很可能被用作
     will be available against which the deductible temporary difference              抵銷且可扣減暫時差異可被動用時）及
     can be utilised) and a deferred tax liability for all deductible and             就與使用權資產及租賃負債相關的所有
     taxable temporary differences associated with the right-of-use assets            可扣減及應課稅暫時差額確認遞延稅項
     and the lease liabilities.                                                       負債。

     The amendments are effective for the Group’s annual reporting period             該等修訂本自本集團於二零二三年一月
     beginning on 1 January 2023. As at 31 December 2022, the carrying                一日起開始的年度報告期間生效。於二
     amounts of right-of-use assets and lease liabilities which are subject           零二二年十二月三十一日，受該等修訂
     to the amendments amounted to HK$1,723,000 and HK$1,853,000                      本影響的使用權資產及租賃負債之賬面
     respectively.                                                                    值分別為1,723,000港元及1,853,000港
                                                                                      元。

     The application of the amendments is not expected to have significant            預期應用該等修訂不會對本集團的財務
     impact on the financial positions and performance of the Group.                  狀況及表現造成重大影響。

                                                                                              準則第9號」）記賬。

           Goodwill                                                                            商譽

           (see accounting policy above) less accumulated impairment                           上文會計政策）減累計減值虧損
           losses, if any.                                                                    （如有）列賬。

           For the purposes of impairment testing, goodwill is allocated                      就減值測試而言，商譽分配到預
           to each of the Group’s cash-generating units (“CGUs”) (or                          期從合併之協同效應中受益的本
           group of CGUs) that is expected to benefit from the synergies                      集團各有關現金產生單位（「現金
           of the combination, which represent the lowest level at which                      產生單位」）
                                                                                                   （ 或現金產生單位之
           the goodwill is monitored for internal management purpose                          組別），而該單位或單位組合為
           and not larger than an operating segment.                                          就內部管理目的的監察商譽的最
                                                                                              低水平且不超過經營分部。

           Existence of significant financing component                                     存在重大融資部分

           promised amount of consideration for the effects of the                          間（明示或暗示）為客戶或本集團

           promise of financing is explicitly stated in the contract or                     訂約方協定之付款條款中，均可
           implied by the payment terms agreed to by the parties to the                     能存在重大融資部分。
           contract.

           component.                                                                       易價。

                                                                                            面值相比較。

           to the asset (or a CGU) for which the estimates of future cash                   值之評估以及估計未來現金流量

            Leases                                                                          租賃
            Definition of a lease                                                           租賃的定義
            A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the                 倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段
            right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of                 時間內控制使用可識別資產的權
            time in exchange for consideration.                                             利，則該合約屬租賃或包含租
                                                                                            賃。

            For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date                     就於首次應用香港財務申報準則
            of initial application of HKFRS 16 or arising from business                     第16號日期或之後訂立或修改
            combinations, the Group assesses whether a contract is                          或源於業務合併的合約而言，本
            or contains a lease based on the definition under HKFRS                         集團根據香港財務申報準則第
            16 at inception, modification date or acquisition date, as                      16號的定義於初始或修改或收
            appropriate. Such contract will not be reassessed unless                        購日期（視乎情況而定）評估該合
            the terms and conditions of the contract are subsequently                       約是否為租賃或包含租賃。除非
            changed.                                                                        合約的條款及條件其後出現變
                                                                                            動，否則有關合約將不予重新評
                                                                                            估。

            The Group as a lessee                                                           本集團作為承租人
            Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                         將代價分配至合約組成部分
            For a contract that contains a lease component and one or                       就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或
            more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group                        多項額外租賃或非租賃組成部分
            allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease                       的合約而言，本集團根據租賃組
            component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of                     成部分的相對獨立價格及非租賃
            the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price of                      組成部分的合計獨立價格基準將
            the non-lease components, including contract for acquisition                    合約代價分配至各項租賃組成部
            of ownership interests of a property which includes both                        分，包括收購含有租賃土地及非
            leasehold land and non-lease building components, unless                        租賃樓宇組成部分的物業的所有
            such allocation cannot be made reliably.                                        權權益的合約，惟有關分配無法
                                                                                            可靠作出則除外。

            The Group applies practical expedient not to separate non-                      本集團應用可行權宜之計不將非
            lease components from lease component, and instead                              租賃組成部分與租賃組成部分分
            account for the lease component and any associated non-                         開，而將租賃組成部分及任何相
            lease components as a single lease component.                                   關非租賃組成部分作為單一租賃
                                                                                            組成部分入賬。

            The Group as a lessor                                                             本集團作為出租人
            Classification and measurement of leases                                          租賃的分類及計量
            Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified as                          本集團作為出租人的租賃分類為
            finance or operating leases. Whenever the terms of the lease                      融資或經營租賃。倘租賃條款將
            transfer substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to                    有關資產擁有權附帶的絕大部分
            ownership of an underlying asset to the lessee, the contract is                   風險及回報轉讓予承租人，合約
            classified as a finance lease. All other leases are classified as                 分類為融資租賃。所有其他租賃
            operating leases.                                                                 均分類為經營租賃。

            Rental income from operating leases is recognised in profit                       經營租賃的租金收益乃按相關租
            or loss on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant                    約協議年期以直線法於損益內確
            lease agreement. Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating                     認。磋商及安排經營租賃過程中
            and arranging an operating lease are added to the carrying                        產生的初始直接成本計入租賃資
            amount of the leased asset, and such costs are recognised as                      產的賬面值，有關成本於租期內
            an expense on a straight-line basis over the lease term except                    按直線法確認為開支，按公平值
            for investment properties measured under fair value model.                        模式計量的投資物業除外。

            Rental income which is derived from the Group’s ordinary                          本集團日常業務過程中產生的租
            course of business are presented as revenue.                                      賃收入呈列為營業額。

            Lease modification                                                                租賃的修改
            Changes in considerations of lease contracts that were not                        不屬於原有條款及條件的租賃合

            as lease modifications, including lease incentives provided                       入賬，包括通過免除或減少租金
            through forgiveness or reduction of rentals.                                      提供的租賃激勵。

            The Group accounts for a modification to an operating lease                       本集團自修改生效日期起將經營
            as a new lease from the effective date of the modification,                       租賃修訂入賬為新租賃，將任何

            lease.                                                                            一部分。

            Taxation                                                                      稅項
            Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                    所得稅開支指現時應繳稅項及遞
            payable and deferred tax.                                                     延稅項之總和。

            The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the                  現時應繳稅項乃按本年度應課稅
            year. Taxable profit differs from profit before taxation because              溢利計算。基於其他年度應課稅
            of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other                  或可扣稅的收入或開支項目，以
            years and items that are never taxable or deductible. The                     及毋須課稅或扣稅項目，因此應
            Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates               課稅溢利與除稅前溢利不同。本
            that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end                    集團即期稅項負債乃按報告期間
            of the reporting period.                                                      結束當日已頒佈或實際已頒佈之
                                                                                          稅率計算。

            Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                           遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表資
            between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the                 產及負債賬面值與計算應課稅溢
            consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax                   利所採用相應稅基間之臨時差額
            bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax                 確認。遞延稅項負債通常會就所
            liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary                有應課稅暫時差額確認。遞延稅
            differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised                     項負債一般會就所有應課稅臨時
            for all deductible temporary difference to the extent that it is              差額確認，而遞延稅項資產一般
            probable that taxable profits will be available against which                 在可能出現可利用臨時差額扣稅
            deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                        之應課稅溢利時就所有可扣減臨
            deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the                 時差額確認。因業務合併以外交
            temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other               易初步確認資產及負債且不影響
            than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in                  應課稅溢利亦不影響會計溢利而
            a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the                 引致之臨時差額，則不會確認該
            accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not              等遞延稅項資產及負債。此外，
            recognised if the temporary difference arises from the initial                不會就因初步確認商譽而引致之
            recognition of goodwill.                                                      臨時差額確認遞延稅項負債。

            attributable to the lease liabilities, the Group applies HKAS              賃交易而言，本集團會對使用權

            of the relevant right-of-use assets and lease liabilities are              用初始確認豁免，故不會確認有
            not recognised due to application of the initial recognition               關使用權資產及租賃負債於初始
            exemption. Temporary differences arising from subsequent                   確認時之暫時差額。其後修訂使
            revision to the carrying amounts of right-of-use assets                    用權資產及租賃負債賬面值、重

            costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected                    收並構成實際利率一部分之費
            life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where                  用、交易成本及其他溢價或折
            appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on                   價）精確折現至初步確認時之賬
            initial recognition.                                                           面淨值之比率。

            The Group performs impairment assessment under expected                      本集團根據預期信貸虧損模式對
            credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets (including trade               按照香港財務申報準則第9號需
            and other receivables, debt instruments at FVTOCI, amounts                   接受減值評估的金融資產（包括
            due from fellow subsidiaries, bills receivables and cash and                 貿易及其他應收賬款、按公平值
            cash equivalents) and financial guarantee contracts which                    計入其他全面收益的債務工具、
            are subject to impairment assessment under HKFRS 9. The                      應收同系附屬公司款項、應收票
            amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect                   據以及現金及現金等價物）以及

                                                                                         期信貸虧損金額於各報告日期更
                                                                                         新，以反映自首次確認以來信貸
                                                                                         風險的變化。

            Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                        全期預期信貸虧損指將因相關工
            all possible default events over the expected life of the                    具預計存續期內所有可能違約事
            relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”)                   件而導致的預期信貸虧損。12
            represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to                   個月預期信貸虧損（「12個月預
            result from default events that are possible within 12 months                期信貸虧損」）指預計因報告日期
            after the reporting date. Assessments are done based on the                  後12個月內可能出現的違約事
            Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors              件導致的全期預期信貸虧損的一
            that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions                部分。評估基於本集團的過往信
            and an assessment of both the current conditions at the                      用損失經驗作出，並就債務人特
            reporting date as well as the forecast of future conditions.                 定因素、整體經濟狀況及報告日
                                                                                         期現行狀況的評估及未來狀況預
                                                                                         測而作出調整。

            individually and/or collectively with appropriate groupings.                     預期信貸虧損進行單項評估及╱

            significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,                   減值撥備，除非自初始確認起信

            (i)    Significant increase in credit risk                                       (i)   信貸風險顯著增加
                   In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                              在評估自初始確認起信

                   compares the risk of a default occurring on the                                 時，本集團將金融工具
                   financial instrument as at the reporting date with the                          於報告日發生違約的風

                   as at the date of initial recognition. In making this                           認日期發生違約的風險

                   and qualitative information that is reasonable and                              估時，本集團考慮合理
                   supportable, including historical experience and                                並有支持的定量及定性
                   forward-looking information that is available without                           資料，包括過往經驗及
                   undue cost or effort.                                                           無需付出不必要的額外
                                                                                                   成本或努力即可獲得的

      In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are                    在應用本集團之會計政策（於附註3載
      described in note 3, the Directors are required to make judgements,                 述）過程中，董事須就未能即時自其他
      estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts of assets                      來源取得資產及負債賬面值，作出判
      and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources.                   斷、估計及假設。該等估計及相關假設
      The estimates and underlying assumptions are based on historical                    按照過往經驗及其他被視為相關的其他
      experience and other factors that are considered to be relevant.                    因素釐定。實際結果可能有別於該等估
      Actual results may differ from these estimates.                                     計。



      The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-                     估計及相關假設會持續審閱。如會計估
      going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in the                計的修改僅影響修改的期間，則在該期
      period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects only that           間確認。如修改影響現時及未來期間，
      period, or in the period of the revision and future periods if revision             則在修改期間及未來期間均會確認。
      affects both current and future periods.

6.    SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                        6.    分部資料
      HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments” (“HKFRS 8”) requires operating                       香 港 財 務 申 報 準 則 第 8 號「 經 營 分 部 」
      segments to be identified on the basis of internal reports about                 （「香港財務申報準則第8號」）要求以主
      components of the Group that are regularly reviewed by Chief                      要營運決策者（「主要營運決策者」）在對
      Operating Decision Maker (“CODM”) in order to allocate resources to               分部作資源分配及評估其表現上所定期
      segments and to assess their performance. Specifically, the Group’s               審閱的有關本集團不同部門之內部報告
      reportable segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into three main                   作為確定經營分部之基準。具體而言，
      operating divisions – (i) manufacturing and sale of laminates; (ii)               於香港財務申報準則第8號下，本集團
      properties; and (iii) investments.                                                之須予呈報之分部分為三個主要經營分
                                                                                        部－(i)製造及銷售覆銅面板；(ii)物業；
                                                                                        及(iii)投資。

      The accounting policies the Group used for segment reporting under               本集團根據香港財務申報準則第8號用
      HKFRS 8 are the same as those used in its HKFRS consolidated                     作分部呈報之會計政策，與其用於香港
      financial statements. Segment results represents the profit earned               財務申報準則之綜合財務報表一致。分
      or loss incurred by each segment with certain items not included                 部業績代表各分部所賺取的溢利或引致
      (unallocated corporate income and expenses, share-based payments                 的虧損，但不包括某些項目（未分配的
      and finance costs). This is the measure reported to the CODM for the             公司收入及支出、股份形式付款及融資
      purpose of resource allocation and performance assessment.                       成本）外。這是報告給主要營運決策者
                                                                                       用於資源分配和績效評估的措施。

9.    INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)                                                  9.   所得稅開支（續）
      The provision of LAT is estimated according to the requirements                      計提土地增值稅是按有關中國稅法及規
      set forth in the relevant PRC tax laws and regulations. LAT has                      則要求估算。按土地增值金額（根據物
      been levied at progressive rates ranging from 30% to 60% on the                      業銷售收入扣去指定直接成本）以累進
      appreciation of land value, represented by the excess of sales                       稅率30%至60%基準繳交土地增值稅。
      proceeds of properties over prescribed direct costs. Prescribed                      指定直接成本界定為土地成本，發展及
      direct costs are defined to include costs of land, development and                   建築成本，及其他關於房產發展的成
      construction costs, as well as certain costs relating to the property                本。按照國家稅務總局之官方公告，銷
      development. According to the State Administration of Taxation’s                     售物業時應暫繳土地增值稅，到房產發
      official circulars, LAT shall be payable provisionally upon sales of                 展完成後才確認所得收益。
      the properties, followed by final ascertainment of the gain at the
      completion of the properties development.

      On 21 March 2018, the Hong Kong Legislative Council passed                           於二零一八年三月二十一日，香港立法
      The Inland Revenue (Amendment) (No. 7) Bill 2017 (the “Bill”) which                  會通過了「二零一七年稅務（修訂）  （ 第7
      introduces the two-tiered profits tax rates regime. The Bill was signed              號）條例草案」 （「條例草案」），該條例草
      into law on 28 March 2018 and was gazetted on the following day.                     案引入了利得稅兩級制。該法案於二零
      Under the two-tiered profits tax rates regime, the first HK$2 million of             一八年三月二十八日簽署成為法律，
      profits of the qualifying group entity will be taxed at 8.25%, and profits           並於次日在憲報刊登。根據利得稅兩級
      above HK$2 million will be taxed at 16.5%. The profits of group                      制，合資格集團實體的首二百萬港元利
      entities not qualifying for the two-tiered profits tax rates regime will             潤將按8.25%徵稅，而超過二百萬港元
      continue to be taxed at a flat rate of 16.5%.                                        的利潤則須按16.5%徵稅。不符合利得
                                                                                           稅兩級制的集團實體的利潤將繼續按
                                                                                           16.5%的固定稅率徵稅。

      The Directors considered the amount involved upon implementation                     董事認為，實施利得稅兩級制所涉及的
      of the two-tiered profits tax rates regime as insignificant to the                   金額與綜合財務報表並無重大關係。兩
      consolidated financial statements. Hong Kong Profits Tax is calculated               個年度的香港利得稅按估計應課稅溢利
      at 16.5% of the estimated assessable profit for both years.                          的16.5%計算。

      Taxation arising in other jurisdictions is calculated at the rates                   其他司法權區之稅項乃按有關司法權區
      prevailing in the relevant jurisdictions.                                            之適用稅率計算。

      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of the        本集團不會因租賃安排而承擔外匯風
      lease arrangement, as all leases are denominated in the respective          險，因為所有租賃均以集團實體各自的

      the property at the end of lease term.                                      權。

      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the Directors     為釐定相關物業的公平值，董事已成立
      have set up a valuation committee, which is headed up by the                估值委員會，由負責本集團財務事宜的
      persons in charge of financial matters of the Group, to determines          人士擔任主席，以就公平值計量釐定適
      the appropriate valuation techniques and inputs for fair value              當的估值技術及輸入數據。
      measurements.

      The Group engages an independent qualified valuer to perform its            本集團委聘獨立合資格估值師估值。管
      valuations. The management works closely with the independent               理層與獨立合資格估值師緊密合作，
      qualified valuer to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and      共同制定模型之適當估值技術及輸入數
      inputs to the model. The persons in charge of financial matters report      據。財務事宜負責人每季向董事匯報管
      the management findings to the Directors every quarter to explain the       理層之發現，闡明資產及負債公平值波
      cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets and liabilities.      動之因由。

            recognised throughout the property construction period until             直至客戶取得竣工物業的控制權
            the customer obtains control of the completed property.                  為止。

            the credit characteristics of the relevant group entities during         度的信貸特徵就款項時間值的影
            the year ended 31 December 2021. As this accrual increases               響作出調整。由於應計款項增加
            the amount of the contract liability during the period of                建築期間的合約負債金額，當竣

            when control of the completed property is transferred to the             增加已確認的營業額金額。
            customer.

      •     Sales of glass epoxy laminates, paper laminates and upstream         •   銷售環氧玻璃纖維覆銅面板、紙
            materials                                                                覆銅面板及上游物料

            When the Group receives a deposit before the production                   本集團會於生產活動開始前收取
            activity commences, this will give rise to contract liabilities           按金，此將於合約開始時導致合
            at the start of a contract, until the revenue recognised when             約負債，直至轉讓貨物控制權

31. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                     31. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will      資本風險管理本集團管理其資本以確保
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to        通過優化債務與股本結存為權益持有人
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.        爭取最高回報，使本集團旗下實體能夠
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.              持續經營。本集團之整體策略與過往年
                                                                                   度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings               誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團之
      and lease liabilities, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity           資本結構包括銀行借貸及租賃負債（扣
      attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share               除現金及現金等價物）及本公司持有人
      capital, other reserves and retained profits as disclosed in the             應佔權益（包括已發行股本、其他儲備
      consolidated statement of changes in equity.                                 及保留溢利）。

      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.           董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and       部分，董事將考慮資本成本，及與各類
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the       別資本相關之風險。董事認為，本集團
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through      將通過支付股息、發行新股、購回股
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as            份、發行新債或者贖回現有債項，以平
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.            衡整體資本結構。

            The Group is exposed to equity price risk and debt price risk           本集團因投資於按公平值計入損

            a special team to monitor the price risk and will consider              層藉持有不同風險之投資組合管

            Credit risk and impairment assessment                                               信貸風險及減值評估
            Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties                      信貸風險指本集團交易對手因違
            default on their contractual obligations resulting in financial                     反其合約義務而導致本集團產生
            losses to the Group. The Group’s maximum exposure                                   財務虧損的風險。倘交易方於報
            to credit risk in the event of the counterparties’ failure to                       告期末未能履行彼等就各類已確
            perform their obligations at the end of the reporting period                        認金融資產之承擔，則本集團須
            in relation to each class of recognised financial assets is the                     承受之最高信貸風險為於綜合財
            carrying amount of those assets as stated in the consolidated                       務狀況表所載該等資產賬面值。
            statement of financial position.

            Trade receivables arising from contracts with customers                             由客戶合約產生的貿易應收賬款
            In order to minimise the credit risk, the Directors has delegated                   為將信貸風險降至最低，董事已
            the management to be responsible for determination of credit                        委派管理層負責制訂信貸限額、
            limits, credit approvals for determination of credit limits, credit                 就制訂信貸限額給予的信貸審
            approvals and other monitoring procedures to ensure that                            批、信貸審批及其他監控程序，
            follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts. In this                         以確保採取跟進措施收回逾期未
            regard, the Directors consider that the Group’s credit risk is                      付之債項。就此而言，本公司董
            significantly reduced.                                                              事認為，本集團之信貸風險已大
                                                                                                幅降低。

            The Group has no significant concentration of credit risk                           本集團並無高度集中之貿易應收
            on trade receivables with exposure spread over a number                             賬款信貸風險，風險由多個交易
            of counterparties and customers. However, the Group is                              方及客戶分攤。然而，本集團的
            exposed to the concentration on geographic segment in the                           風險集中於中國之地區分部。於
            PRC. At 31 December 2022, approximately 89% (2021: 86%)                             二零二二年十二月三十一日，本
            of the Group’s trade receivables are arising from the PRC.                          集團約89%（二零二一年：86%）
                                                                                                之貿易應收賬款源自中國。

            Except for credit-impaired trade receivables which are                              除個別進行減值評估的信貸減值
            assessed for impairment individually, the remaining trade                           貿易應收賬款外，其餘貿易應收
            receivables are grouped based on shared credit risk                                 賬款參照本集團內部信貸評級，
            characteristics by reference to the Group’s internal credit                         基於共同信貸風險特徵加以分
            ratings. Details of the quantitative disclosures are set out                        組。定量披露詳情載於本附註下
            below in this note.                                                                 文。
